TF-IDF index building with multiprocessing\n2. Batch search with parallel query processing\n3. Load-balanced work distribution\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup on index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup on search with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _TokenizationJob:\n    \"\"\"Work unit for document tokenization.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_id: int\n    text: str\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _TokenizationResult:\n    \"\"\"Result of document tokenization.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_id: int\n    tokens: list[str]\n    term_freqs: dict[str, float]\n    terms_set: set[str]\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _SearchJob:\n    \"\"\"Work unit for query search.\"\"\"\n\n    query_id: int\n    query: str\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions (must be at module level for multiprocessing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_document(job: _TokenizationJob) -> _TokenizationResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: tokenize a single document.\n\n    This function is called by worker processes in the multiprocessing pool.\n    It tokenizes text and computes term frequencies for a document.\n\n    Args:\n        job: TokenizationJob containing document ID and text\n\n    Returns:\n        TokenizationResult with tokens and term frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    tokens = tokenize(job.text)\n    term_freqs = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n    terms_set = set(term_freqs.keys())\n\n    return _TokenizationResult(\n        doc_id=job.doc_id,\n        tokens=tokens,\n        term_freqs=term_freqs,\n        terms_set=terms_set,\n    )\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch_worker(args: tuple[list[int], list[str], dict, dict, dict, dict, int]) -> list[tuple[int, list[SearchResult]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: process a batch of queries.\n\n    This function is called by worker processes to search for multiple queries at once,\n    reducing IPC overhead and improving cache locality.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query_ids, queries, idf_dict, doc_vectors, doc_norms, doc_titles, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of (query_id, list of SearchResult objects) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    query_ids, queries, idf_dict, doc_vectors, doc_norms, doc_titles, top_k = args\n\n    batch_results = []\n\n    for query_id, query in zip(query_ids, queries):\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            batch_results.append((query_id, []))\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in idf_dict:\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            batch_results.append((query_id, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents and compute similarity\n        candidate_scores = {}\n\n        for term in query_vector:\n            query_tfidf = query_vector[term]\n            # Iterate through all documents that have this term\n            for doc_id, doc_vec in doc_vectors.items():\n                if term in doc_vec:\n                    if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                        candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n                    candidate_scores[doc_id] += query_tfidf * doc_vec[term]\n\n        # Normalize by norms and get top-k\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id, dot_product in candidate_scores.items():\n            doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n            if doc_norm > 0:\n                similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n                scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        # Build result objects\n        results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            results.append(SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            ))\n\n        batch_results.append((query_id, results))\n\n    return batch_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes the CPU-bound operations:\n    1. Document tokenization (embarrassingly parallel)\n    2. TF-IDF vector computation (embarrassingly parallel)\n\n    Global steps (document frequencies, IDF) are computed sequentially\n    on the main process.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of chunks for worker pool (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 1: Parallel tokenization\n    # ========================================================================\n    tokenization_jobs = [\n        _TokenizationJob(doc_id=doc.doc_id, text=doc.title + \" \" + doc.content)\n        for doc in documents\n    ]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        tokenization_results = pool.imap_unordered(\n            _tokenize_document,\n            tokenization_jobs,\n            chunksize=chunk_size\n        )\n\n        for result in tokenization_results:\n            doc_term_freqs[result.doc_id] = result.term_freqs\n            doc_terms[result.doc_id] = result.terms_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(result.terms_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 2: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - small overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 4: Parallel TF-IDF vector computation and inverted index building\n    # ========================================================================\n    # We'll compute these in parallel by processing documents in chunks\n\n    def compute_tfidf_vectors(docs_to_process):\n        \"\"\"Helper to compute TFIDF vectors for a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n        batch_vectors = {}\n        batch_norms = {}\n\n        for doc_id in docs_to_process:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            batch_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            batch_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n        return batch_vectors, batch_norms\n\n    # Process all documents (can be parallelized further if needed)\n    all_vectors, all_norms = compute_tfidf_vectors(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    index.doc_vectors = all_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = all_norms\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 5: Build inverted index with sorted posting lists\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform batch similarity search in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes query processing across multiple workers.\n    Each worker independently computes similarity scores for its assigned queries.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Prepare document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Partition queries into batches for each worker\n    # Use larger batches to amortize worker startup overhead\n    num_batches = max(num_workers, min(num_workers * 2, len(queries)))\n    batch_size = max(1, (len(queries) + num_batches - 1) // num_batches)\n\n    search_jobs = []\n    for batch_start in range(0, len(queries), batch_size):\n        batch_end = min(batch_start + batch_size, len(queries))\n        batch_ids = list(range(batch_start, batch_end))\n        batch_queries = queries[batch_start:batch_end]\n\n        search_jobs.append((\n            batch_ids,\n            batch_queries,\n            index.idf,\n            index.doc_vectors,\n            index.doc_norms,\n            doc_titles,\n            top_k\n        ))\n\n    # Process query batches in parallel\n    results_by_query = [None] * len(queries)\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.imap_unordered(\n            _search_query_batch_worker,\n            search_jobs\n        )\n\n        for batch_result_list in batch_results:\n            for query_id, results in batch_result_list:\n                results_by_query[query_id] = results\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_by_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Utility Functions for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef verify_index_equality(index1: TFIDFIndex, index2: TFIDFIndex, tolerance: float = 1e-6) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify that two indices are equivalent (for correctness testing).\n\n    Args:\n        index1: First index\n        index2: Second index\n        tolerance: Floating point tolerance for score comparison\n\n    Returns:\n        True if indices are equivalent\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check basic properties\n    if index1.num_documents != index2.num_documents:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.vocabulary != index2.vocabulary:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.document_frequencies != index2.document_frequencies:\n        return False\n\n    # Check IDF scores\n    for term in index1.idf:\n        if term not in index2.idf:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.idf[term] - index2.idf[term]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    # Check document vectors\n    if set(index1.doc_vectors.keys()) != set(index2.doc_vectors.keys()):\n        return False\n\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_vectors:\n        vec1 = index1.doc_vectors[doc_id]\n        vec2 = index2.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n\n        if set(vec1.keys()) != set(vec2.keys()):\n            return False\n\n        for term in vec1:\n            if abs(vec1[term] - vec2.get(term, 0)) > tolerance:\n                return False\n\n    # Check document norms\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_norms:\n        if doc_id not in index2.doc_norms:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.doc_norms[doc_id] - index2.doc_norms[doc_id]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    return True\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Testing and Benchmarking\n# ============================================================================\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import sys\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    # ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_document(job: _TokenizationJob) -> _TokenizationResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: tokenize a single document.\n\n    This function is called by worker processes in the multiprocessing pool.\n    It tokenizes text and computes term frequencies for a document.\n\n    Args:\n        job: TokenizationJob containing document ID and text\n\n    Returns:\n        TokenizationResult with tokens and term frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    tokens = tokenize(job.text)\n    term_freqs = terms_set=terms_set,\n    )\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch_worker(args: tuple[list[int], list[str], dict, dict, dict, dict, int]) -> list[tuple[int, list[SearchResult]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: process a batch of queries.\n\n    This function is called by worker processes to search for multiple queries at once,\n    reducing IPC overhead and improving cache locality.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query_ids, queries, idf_dict, doc_vectors, doc_norms, doc_titles, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of (query_id, list of SearchResult objects) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    query_ids, queries, idf_dict, doc_vectors, doc_norms, doc_titles, top_k = args\n\n    batch_results = []\n\n    for query_id, query in zip(query_ids, queries):\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            batch_results.append((query_id, []))\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in idf_dict:\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            batch_results.append((query_id, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents and compute similarity\n        candidate_scores = {}\n\n        for term in query_vector:\n            query_tfidf = query_vector[term]\n            # Iterate through all documents that have this term\n            for doc_id, doc_vec in doc_vectors.items():\n                if term in doc_vec:\n                    if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                        candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n            if doc_norm > 0:\n                similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n                scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        # Build result objects\n        results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            results.append(SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id,\n                score=score,\n                title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            ))\n\n        batch_results.append((query_id, results))\n\n    return batch_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes the CPU-bound operations:\n    1. Document tokenization (embarrassingly parallel)\n    2. TF-IDF vector computation (embarrassingly parallel)\n\n    Global steps (document frequencies, IDF) are computed sequentially\n    on the main process.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of chunks for worker pool (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 1: Parallel tokenization\n    # ========================================================================\n    tokenization_jobs = [\n        _TokenizationJob(doc_id=doc.doc_id, text=doc.title + \" \" + doc.content)\n        for doc in documents\n    ]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        tokenization_results = pool.imap_unordered(\n            _tokenize_document,\n            tokenization_jobs,\n            chunksize=chunk_size\n        )\n\n        for result in tokenization_results:\n            doc_term_freqs[result.doc_id] = result.term_freqs\n            doc_terms[result.doc_id] = result.terms_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(result.terms_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 2: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - small overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 4: Parallel TF-IDF vector computation and inverted index building\n    # ========================================================================\n    # We'll compute these in parallel by processing documents in chunks\n\n    def compute_tfidf_vectors(docs_to_process):\n        \"\"\"Helper to compute TFIDF vectors for a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n        batch_vectors = {}\n        batch_norms = {}\n\n        for doc_id in docs_to_process:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            batch_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            batch_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n        return batch_vectors, batch_norms\n\n    # Process all documents (can be parallelized further if needed)\n    all_vectors, all_norms = compute_tfidf_vectors(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    index.doc_vectors = all_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = all_norms\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 5: Build inverted index with sorted posting lists\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform batch similarity search in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes query processing across multiple workers.\n    Each worker independently computes similarity scores for its assigned queries.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Check basic properties\n    if index1.num_documents != index2.num_documents:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.vocabulary != index2.vocabulary:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.document_frequencies != index2.document_frequencies:\n        return False\n\n    # Check IDF scores\n    for term in index1.idf:\n        if term not in index2.idf:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.idf[term] - index2.idf[term]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    # Check document vectors\n    if set(index1.doc_vectors.keys()) != set(index2.doc_vectors.keys()):\n        return False\n\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_vectors:\n        vec1 = index1.doc_vectors[doc_id]\n        vec2 = index2.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n\n        if set(vec1.keys()) != set(vec2.keys()):\n            return False\n\n        for term in vec1:\n            if abs(vec1[term] - vec2.get(term, 0)) > tolerance:\n                return False\n\n    # TF-IDF index building with multiprocessing\n2. Batch search with parallel query processing\n3. Load-balanced work distribution\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup on index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup on search with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, ProcessPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _TokenizationJob:\n    \"\"\"Work unit for document tokenization.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_id: terms_set: set[str]\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _SearchJob:\n    \"\"\"Work unit for query search.\"\"\"\n\n    query_id: int\n    query: str\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions (must be at module level for multiprocessing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_document(job: _TokenizationJob) -> _TokenizationResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: tokenize a single document.\n\n    This function is called by worker processes in the multiprocessing pool.\n    It tokenizes text and computes term frequencies for a document.\n\n    Args:\n        job: TokenizationJob containing document ID and text\n\n    Returns:\n        TokenizationResult with tokens and term frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    tokens = tokenize(job.text)\n    term_freqs = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n    terms_set = set(term_freqs.keys())\n\n    return _TokenizationResult(\n        doc_id=job.doc_id,\n        tokens=tokens,\n        term_freqs=term_freqs,\n        terms_set=terms_set,\n    )\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(query_id: int, query: str, idf_dict: dict, doc_vectors: dict, doc_norms: dict, doc_titles: dict, top_k: int) -> tuple[int, list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: process a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        query_id: ID of the query\n        query: Query string\n        idf_dict: IDF dictionary\n        doc_vectors: Document vectors\n        doc_norms: Document norms\n        doc_titles: Document title mapping\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (query_id, list of SearchResult objects)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf_dict:\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents and compute similarity\n    candidate_scores = {}\n\n    for term in query_vector:\n        query_tfidf = query_vector[term]\n        # Iterate through all documents that have this term\n        for doc_id, doc_vec in doc_vectors.items():\n            if term in doc_vec:\n                if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                    candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n                candidate_scores[doc_id] += query_tfidf * doc_vec[term]\n\n    # Normalize by norms and get top-k\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id, dot_product in candidate_scores.items():\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n        if doc_norm > 0:\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        ))\n\n    return query_id, results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes the CPU-bound operations:\n    1. Document tokenization (embarrassingly parallel)\n    2. TF-IDF vector computation (embarrassingly parallel)\n\n    Global steps (document frequencies, IDF) are computed sequentially\n    on the main process.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of chunks for worker pool (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 1: Parallel tokenization\n    # ========================================================================\n    tokenization_jobs = [\n        _TokenizationJob(doc_id=doc.doc_id, text=doc.title + \" \" + doc.content)\n        for doc in documents\n    ]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        tokenization_results = pool.imap_unordered(\n            _tokenize_document,\n            tokenization_jobs,\n            chunksize=chunk_size\n        )\n\n        for result in tokenization_results:\n            doc_term_freqs[result.doc_id] = result.term_freqs\n            doc_terms[result.doc_id] = result.terms_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(result.terms_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 2: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - small overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 4: Parallel TF-IDF vector computation and inverted index building\n    # ========================================================================\n    # We'll compute these in parallel by processing documents in chunks\n\n    def compute_tfidf_vectors(docs_to_process):\n        \"\"\"Helper to compute TFIDF vectors for a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n        batch_vectors = {}\n        batch_norms = {}\n\n        for doc_id in docs_to_process:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            batch_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            batch_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n        return batch_vectors, batch_norms\n\n    # Process all documents (can be parallelized further if needed)\n    all_vectors, all_norms = compute_tfidf_vectors(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    index.doc_vectors = all_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = all_norms\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 5: Build inverted index with sorted posting lists\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform batch similarity search in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes query processing across multiple workers.\n    Each worker independently computes similarity scores for its assigned queries.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Process query batches in parallel\n    results_by_query = [None] * len(queries)\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.imap_unordered(\n            _search_query_batch_worker,\n            search_jobs\n        )\n\n        for batch_result_list in batch_results:\n            for query_id, results in batch_result_list:\n                results_by_query[query_id] = results\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_by_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Utility Functions for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef verify_index_equality(index1: TFIDFIndex, index2: TFIDFIndex, tolerance: float = 1e-6) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify that two indices are equivalent (for correctness testing).\n\n    Args:\n        index1: First index\n        index2: Second index\n        tolerance: Floating point tolerance for score comparison\n\n    Returns:\n        True if indices are equivalent\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check basic properties\n    if index1.num_documents != index2.num_documents:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.vocabulary != index2.vocabulary:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.document_frequencies != index2.document_frequencies:\n        return False\n\n    # Check IDF scores\n    for term in index1.idf:\n        if term not in index2.idf:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.idf[term] - index2.idf[term]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    # Check document vectors\n    if set(index1.doc_vectors.keys()) != set(index2.doc_vectors.keys()):\n        return False\n\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_vectors:\n        vec1 = index1.doc_vectors[doc_id]\n        vec2 = index2.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n\n        if set(vec1.keys()) != set(vec2.keys()):\n            return False\n\n        for term in vec1:\n            if abs(vec1[term] - vec2.get(term, 0)) > tolerance:\n                return False\n\n    # Check document norms\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_norms:\n        if doc_id not in index2.doc_norms:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.doc_norms[doc_id] - index2.doc_norms[doc_id]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    return True\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Testing and Benchmarking\n# ============================================================================\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import sys\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    # TF-IDF index building with multiprocessing\n2. Batch search with parallel query processing\n3. Load-balanced work distribution\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup on index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup on search with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, ProcessPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _TokenizationJob:\n    \"\"\"Work unit for document tokenization.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_id: int\n    text: str\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _TokenizationResult:\n    \"\"\"Result of document tokenization.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_id: int\n    tokens: list[str]\n    term_freqs: dict[str, float]\n    terms_set: set[str]\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _SearchJob:\n    \"\"\"Work unit for query search.\"\"\"\n\n    query_id: int\n    query: str\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions (must be at module level for multiprocessing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_document(job: _TokenizationJob) -> _TokenizationResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: tokenize a single document.\n\n    This function is called by worker processes in the multiprocessing pool.\n    It tokenizes text and computes term frequencies for a document.\n\n    Args:\n        job: TokenizationJob containing document ID and text\n\n    Returns:\n        TokenizationResult with tokens and term frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    tokens = tokenize(job.text)\n    term_freqs = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n    terms_set = set(term_freqs.keys())\n\n    return _TokenizationResult(\n        doc_id=job.doc_id,\n        tokens=tokens,\n        term_freqs=term_freqs,\n        terms_set=terms_set,\n    )\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(query_id: int, query: str, idf_dict: dict, doc_vectors: dict, doc_norms: dict, doc_titles: dict, top_k: int) -> tuple[int, list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: process a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        query_id: ID of the query\n        query: Query string\n        idf_dict: IDF dictionary\n        doc_vectors: Document vectors\n        doc_norms: Document norms\n        doc_titles: Document title mapping\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (query_id, list of SearchResult objects)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf_dict:\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents and compute similarity\n    candidate_scores = {}\n\n    for term in query_vector:\n        query_tfidf = query_vector[term]\n        # Iterate through all documents that have this term\n        for doc_id, doc_vec in doc_vectors.items():\n            if term in doc_vec:\n                if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                    candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n                candidate_scores[doc_id] += query_tfidf * doc_vec[term]\n\n    # Normalize by norms and get top-k\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id, dot_product in candidate_scores.items():\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n        if doc_norm > 0:\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        ))\n\n    return query_id, results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes the CPU-bound operations:\n    1. Document tokenization (embarrassingly parallel)\n    2. TF-IDF vector computation (embarrassingly parallel)\n\n    Global steps (document frequencies, IDF) are computed sequentially\n    on the main process.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of chunks for worker pool (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 1: Parallel tokenization\n    # ========================================================================\n    tokenization_jobs = [\n        _TokenizationJob(doc_id=doc.doc_id, text=doc.title + \" \" + doc.content)\n        for doc in documents\n    ]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        tokenization_results = pool.imap_unordered(\n            _tokenize_document,\n            tokenization_jobs,\n            chunksize=chunk_size\n        )\n\n        for result in tokenization_results:\n            doc_term_freqs[result.doc_id] = result.term_freqs\n            doc_terms[result.doc_id] = result.terms_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(result.terms_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 2: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - small overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 4: Parallel TF-IDF vector computation and inverted index building\n    # ========================================================================\n    # We'll compute these in parallel by processing documents in chunks\n\n    def compute_tfidf_vectors(docs_to_process):\n        \"\"\"Helper to compute TFIDF vectors for a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n        batch_vectors = {}\n        batch_norms = {}\n\n        for doc_id in docs_to_process:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            batch_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            batch_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n        return batch_vectors, batch_norms\n\n    # Process all documents (can be parallelized further if needed)\n    all_vectors, all_norms = compute_tfidf_vectors(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    index.doc_vectors = all_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = all_norms\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 5: Build inverted index with sorted posting lists\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform batch similarity search in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes query processing across multiple workers.\n    Each worker independently computes similarity scores for its assigned queries.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Prepare document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Use ThreadPoolExecutor for search (Python GIL allows thread parallelism for I/O-bound CPU tasks)\n    results_by_query = [None] * len(queries)\n\n    def search_wrapper(query_id_query_pair):\n        query_id, query = query_id_query_pair\n        return _search_single_query(query_id, query, index.idf, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms, doc_titles, top_k)\n\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        query_pairs = list(enumerate(queries))\n        search_results = executor.map(search_wrapper, query_pairs)\n\n        for query_id, results in search_results:\n            results_by_query[query_id] = results\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_by_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Utility Functions for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef verify_index_equality(index1: TFIDFIndex, index2: TFIDFIndex, tolerance: float = 1e-6) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify that two indices are equivalent (for correctness testing).\n\n    Args:\n        index1: First index\n        index2: Second index\n        tolerance: Floating point tolerance for score comparison\n\n    Returns:\n        True if indices are equivalent\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check basic properties\n    if index1.num_documents != index2.num_documents:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.vocabulary != index2.vocabulary:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.document_frequencies != index2.document_frequencies:\n        return False\n\n    # Check IDF scores\n    for term in index1.idf:\n        if term not in index2.idf:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.idf[term] - index2.idf[term]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    # Check document vectors\n    if set(index1.doc_vectors.keys()) != set(index2.doc_vectors.keys()):\n        return False\n\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_vectors:\n        vec1 = index1.doc_vectors[doc_id]\n        vec2 = index2.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n\n        if set(vec1.keys()) != set(vec2.keys()):\n            return False\n\n        for term in vec1:\n            if abs(vec1[term] - vec2.get(term, 0)) > tolerance:\n                return False\n\n    # Check document norms\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_norms:\n        if doc_id not in index2.doc_norms:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.doc_norms[doc_id] - index2.doc_norms[doc_id]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    return True\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Testing and Benchmarking\n# ============================================================================\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import sys\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    # ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions (must be at module level for multiprocessing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_document(job: _TokenizationJob) -> _TokenizationResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: tokenize a single document.\n\n    This function is called by worker processes in the multiprocessing pool.\n    It tokenizes text and computes term frequencies for a document.\n\n    Args:\n        job: TokenizationJob containing document ID and text\n\n    Returns:\n        TokenizationResult with tokens and term frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    tokens = tokenize(job.text)\n    term_freqs = terms_set=terms_set,\n    )\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(query_id: int, query: str, idf_dict: dict, doc_vectors: dict, doc_norms: dict, doc_titles: dict, top_k: int) -> tuple[int, list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: process a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        query_id: ID of the query\n        query: Query string\n        idf_dict: IDF dictionary\n        doc_vectors: Document vectors\n        doc_norms: Document norms\n        doc_titles: Document title mapping\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (query_id, list of SearchResult objects)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf_dict:\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents and compute similarity\n    candidate_scores = {}\n\n    for term in query_vector:\n        query_tfidf = query_vector[term]\n        # Iterate through all documents that have this term\n        for doc_id, doc_vec in doc_vectors.items():\n            if term in doc_vec:\n                if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                    candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n                candidate_scores[doc_id] += query_tfidf * doc_vec[term]\n\n    # Normalize by norms and get top-k\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id, dot_product in candidate_scores.items():\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n        if doc_norm > 0:\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        ))\n\n    return query_id, results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes the CPU-bound operations:\n    1. Document tokenization (embarrassingly parallel)\n    2. TF-IDF vector computation (embarrassingly parallel)\n\n    Global steps (document frequencies, IDF) are computed sequentially\n    on the main process.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of chunks for worker pool (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 1: Parallel tokenization using ProcessPoolExecutor\n    # ========================================================================\n    tokenization_jobs = [\n        _TokenizationJob(doc_id=doc.doc_id, text=doc.title + \" \" + doc.content)\n        for doc in documents\n    ]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        tokenization_results = executor.map(_tokenize_document, tokenization_jobs, chunksize=chunk_size)\n\n        for result in tokenization_results:\n            doc_term_freqs[result.doc_id] = result.term_freqs\n            doc_terms[result.doc_id] = result.terms_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(result.terms_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 2: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - small overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 4: Parallel TF-IDF vector computation and inverted index building\n    # ========================================================================\n    # We'll compute these in parallel by processing documents in chunks\n\n    def compute_tfidf_vectors(docs_to_process):\n        \"\"\"Helper to compute TFIDF vectors for a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n        batch_vectors = {}\n        batch_norms = {}\n\n        for doc_id in docs_to_process:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            batch_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            batch_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n        return batch_vectors, batch_norms\n\n    # Process all documents (can be parallelized further if needed)\n    all_vectors, all_norms = compute_tfidf_vectors(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    index.doc_vectors = all_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = all_norms\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 5: Build inverted index with sorted posting lists\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform batch similarity search in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes query processing across multiple workers.\n    Each worker independently computes similarity scores for its assigned queries.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Check basic properties\n    if index1.num_documents != index2.num_documents:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.vocabulary != index2.vocabulary:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.document_frequencies != index2.document_frequencies:\n        return False\n\n    # Check IDF scores\n    for term in index1.idf:\n        if term not in index2.idf:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.idf[term] - index2.idf[term]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    # Check document vectors\n    if set(index1.doc_vectors.keys()) != set(index2.doc_vectors.keys()):\n        return False\n\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_vectors:\n        vec1 = index1.doc_vectors[doc_id]\n        vec2 = index2.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n\n        if set(vec1.keys()) != set(vec2.keys()):\n            return False\n\n        for term in vec1:\n            if abs(vec1[term] - vec2.get(term, 0)) > tolerance:\n                return False\n\n    # Check document norms\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_norms:\n        if doc_id not in index2.doc_norms:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.doc_norms[doc_id] - index2.doc_norms[doc_id]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    return True\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Testing and Benchmarking\n# ============================================================================\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import sys\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    # Optimized\n\nThis module provides a parallel implementation of:\n1. TF-IDF index building with multiprocessing\n2. Batch search with parallel query processing\n3. Load-balanced work distribution\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup on index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup on search with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, ProcessPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _TokenizationJob:\n    \"\"\"Work unit for document tokenization.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_id: terms_set: set[str]\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _SearchJob:\n    \"\"\"Work unit for query search.\"\"\"\n\n    query_id: int\n    query: str\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions (must be at module level for multiprocessing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_document(job: _TokenizationJob) -> _TokenizationResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: tokenize a single document.\n\n    This function is called by worker processes in the multiprocessing pool.\n    It tokenizes text and computes term frequencies for a document.\n\n    Args:\n        job: TokenizationJob containing document ID and text\n\n    Returns:\n        TokenizationResult with tokens and term frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    tokens = tokenize(job.text)\n    term_freqs = terms_set=terms_set,\n    )\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(query_id: int, query: str, idf_dict: dict, doc_vectors: dict, doc_norms: dict, doc_titles: dict, top_k: int) -> tuple[int, list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: process a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        query_id: ID of the query\n        query: Query string\n        idf_dict: IDF dictionary\n        doc_vectors: Document vectors\n        doc_norms: Document norms\n        doc_titles: Document title mapping\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (query_id, list of SearchResult objects)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf_dict:\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents and compute similarity\n    candidate_scores = {}\n\n    for term in query_vector:\n        query_tfidf = query_vector[term]\n        # Iterate through all documents that have this term\n        for doc_id, doc_vec in doc_vectors.items():\n            if term in doc_vec:\n                if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                    candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n                candidate_scores[doc_id] += query_tfidf * doc_vec[term]\n\n    # Normalize by norms and get top-k\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id, dot_product in candidate_scores.items():\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n        if doc_norm > 0:\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        ))\n\n    return query_id, results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes the CPU-bound operations:\n    1. Document tokenization (embarrassingly parallel)\n    2. TF-IDF vector computation (embarrassingly parallel)\n\n    Global steps (document frequencies, IDF) are computed sequentially\n    on the main process.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of chunks for worker pool (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 1: Parallel tokenization using ProcessPoolExecutor\n    # ========================================================================\n    tokenization_jobs = [\n        _TokenizationJob(doc_id=doc.doc_id, text=doc.title + \" \" + doc.content)\n        for doc in documents\n    ]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        tokenization_results = executor.map(_tokenize_document, tokenization_jobs, chunksize=chunk_size)\n\n        for result in tokenization_results:\n            doc_term_freqs[result.doc_id] = result.term_freqs\n            doc_terms[result.doc_id] = result.terms_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(result.terms_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 2: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - small overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 4: Parallel TF-IDF vector computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    @dataclass\n    class _TFIDFComputeJob:\n        doc_ids: list[int]\n        idf_dict: dict\n\n    def compute_tfidf_batch(job: _TFIDFComputeJob) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Compute TFIDF vectors and norms for a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n        batch_data = {}\n        for doc_id in job.doc_ids:\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_vector = {}\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                tfidf = tf * job.idf_dict[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            batch_data[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n\n        return batch_data\n\n    # Create batch jobs for parallel processing\n    doc_ids_list = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    batch_size_tfidf = max(1, (len(doc_ids_list) + num_workers - 1) // num_workers)\n    tfidf_jobs = []\n\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids_list), batch_size_tfidf):\n        batch_ids = doc_ids_list[i:i + batch_size_tfidf]\n        tfidf_jobs.append(_TFIDFComputeJob(doc_ids=batch_ids, idf_dict=index.idf))\n\n    all_vectors = {}\n    all_norms = {}\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        batch_results = executor.map(compute_tfidf_batch, tfidf_jobs)\n        for batch_data in batch_results:\n            for doc_id, (vec, norm) in batch_data.items():\n                all_vectors[doc_id] = vec\n                all_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    index.doc_vectors = all_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = all_norms\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 5: Build inverted index (sequential - acceptable overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform batch similarity search in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes query processing across multiple workers.\n    Each worker independently computes similarity scores for its assigned queries.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Prepare document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Use ThreadPoolExecutor for search (Python GIL allows thread parallelism for I/O-bound CPU tasks)\n    results_by_query = [None] * len(queries)\n\n    def search_wrapper(query_id_query_pair):\n        query_id, query = query_id_query_pair\n        return _search_single_query(query_id, query, index.idf, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms, doc_titles, top_k)\n\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        query_pairs = list(enumerate(queries))\n        search_results = executor.map(search_wrapper, query_pairs)\n\n        for query_id, results in search_results:\n            results_by_query[query_id] = results\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_by_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Utility Functions for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef verify_index_equality(index1: TFIDFIndex, index2: TFIDFIndex, tolerance: float = 1e-6) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify that two indices are equivalent (for correctness testing).\n\n    Args:\n        index1: First index\n        index2: Second index\n        tolerance: Floating point tolerance for score comparison\n\n    Returns:\n        True if indices are equivalent\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check basic properties\n    if index1.num_documents != index2.num_documents:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.vocabulary != index2.vocabulary:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.document_frequencies != index2.document_frequencies:\n        return False\n\n    # Check IDF scores\n    for term in index1.idf:\n        if term not in index2.idf:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.idf[term] - index2.idf[term]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    # Check document vectors\n    if set(index1.doc_vectors.keys()) != set(index2.doc_vectors.keys()):\n        return False\n\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_vectors:\n        vec1 = index1.doc_vectors[doc_id]\n        vec2 = index2.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n\n        if set(vec1.keys()) != set(vec2.keys()):\n            return False\n\n        for term in vec1:\n            if abs(vec1[term] - vec2.get(term, 0)) > tolerance:\n                return False\n\n    #
