tf_dict = job.doc_term_freqs_dict.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * job.idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        batch_data[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n\n    return batch_data\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(query_id: int, query: str, idf_dict: dict, doc_vectors: dict, doc_norms: dict, doc_titles: dict, top_k: int) -> tuple[int, list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: process a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        query_id: ID of the query\n        query: Query string\n        idf_dict: IDF dictionary\n        doc_vectors: Document vectors\n        doc_norms: Document norms\n        doc_titles: Document title mapping\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (query_id, list of SearchResult objects)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf_dict:\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents and compute similarity\n    candidate_scores = {}\n\n    for term in query_vector:\n        query_tfidf = query_vector[term]\n        # Iterate through all documents that have this term\n        for doc_id, doc_vec in doc_vectors.items():\n            if term in doc_vec:\n                if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                    candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n                candidate_scores[doc_id] += query_tfidf * doc_vec[term]\n\n    # Normalize by norms and get top-k\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id, dot_product in candidate_scores.items():\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n        if doc_norm > 0:\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        ))\n\n    return query_id, results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes the CPU-bound operations:\n    1. Document tokenization (embarrassingly parallel)\n    2. TF-IDF vector computation (embarrassingly parallel)\n\n    Global steps (document frequencies, IDF) are computed sequentially\n    on the main process.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of chunks for worker pool (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 1: Parallel tokenization with optimized chunking\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Use dynamic chunking to balance overhead vs. granularity\n    # For large datasets, use larger chunks; for small, use smaller\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n    optimal_chunk_size = max(50, min(chunk_size, num_docs // (num_workers * 2)))\n\n    tokenization_jobs = [\n        _TokenizationJob(doc_id=doc.doc_id, text=doc.title + \" \" + doc.content)\n        for doc in documents\n    ]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    # Use submit + as_completed for better load distribution\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        # Submit all jobs at once to reduce latency\n        futures = {\n            executor.submit(_tokenize_document, job): job.doc_id\n            for job in tokenization_jobs\n        }\n\n        # Process results as they complete to start secondary work early\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            result = future.result()\n            doc_term_freqs[result.doc_id] = result.term_freqs\n            doc_terms[result.doc_id] = result.terms_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(result.terms_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 2: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - small overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 4: Build document vectors and compute norms (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 5: Build inverted index (sequential - acceptable overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform batch similarity search in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes query processing across multiple workers.\n    Each worker independently computes similarity scores for its assigned queries.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Prepare document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Use ThreadPoolExecutor for search parallelization\n    # For large query batches, use threading to avoid IPC overhead\n    # For small query batches, sequential is often faster\n    results_by_query = [None] * len(queries)\n\n    if len(queries) > 20:\n        # For larger batches, use threading\n        def search_wrapper(query_id_query_pair):\n            query_id, query = query_id_query_pair\n            return _search_single_query(query_id, query, index.idf, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms, doc_titles, top_k)\n\n        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            query_pairs = list(enumerate(queries))\n            search_results = executor.map(search_wrapper, query_pairs)\n\n            for query_id, results in search_results:\n                results_by_query[query_id] = results\n    else:\n        # For small batches, sequential is faster due to lower overhead\n        for query_id, query in enumerate(queries):\n            _, results = _search_single_query(query_id, query, index.idf, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms, doc_titles, top_k)\n            results_by_query[query_id] = results\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_by_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Utility Functions for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef verify_index_equality(index1: TFIDFIndex, index2: TFIDFIndex, tolerance: float = 1e-6) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify that two indices are equivalent (for correctness testing).\n\n    Args:\n        index1: First index\n        index2: Second index\n        tolerance: Floating point tolerance for score comparison\n\n    Returns:\n        True if indices are equivalent\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check basic properties\n    if index1.num_documents != index2.num_documents:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.vocabulary != index2.vocabulary:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.document_frequencies != index2.document_frequencies:\n        return False\n\n    # Check IDF scores\n    for term in index1.idf:\n        if term not in index2.idf:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.idf[term] - index2.idf[term]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    # Check document vectors\n    if set(index1.doc_vectors.keys()) != set(index2.doc_vectors.keys()):\n        return False\n\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_vectors:\n        vec1 = index1.doc_vectors[doc_id]\n        vec2 = index2.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n\n        if set(vec1.keys()) != set(vec2.keys()):\n            return False\n\n        for term in vec1:\n            if abs(vec1[term] - vec2.get(term, 0)) > tolerance:\n                return False\n\n    # Check document norms\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_norms:\n        if doc_id not in index2.doc_norms:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.doc_norms[doc_id] - index2.doc_norms[doc_id]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    return True\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Testing and Benchmarking\n# ============================================================================\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import sys\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    # SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_document(job: _TokenizationJob) -> _TokenizationResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: tokenize a single document.\n\n    This function is called by worker processes in the multiprocessing pool.\n    It tokenizes text and computes term frequencies for a document.\n\n    Args:\n        job: TokenizationJob containing document ID and text\n\n    Returns:\n        TokenizationResult with tokens and term frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    tokens = tokenize(job.text)\n    term_freqs = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n    terms_set = set(term_freqs.keys())\n\n    return _TokenizationResult(\n        doc_id=job.doc_id,\n        tokens=tokens,\n        term_freqs=term_freqs,\n        terms_set=terms_set,\n    )\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass _TFIDFComputeJob:\n    \"\"\"Work unit for TF-IDF vector computation.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_ids: list[int]\n    idf_dict: dict\n    doc_term_freqs_dict: dict\n\n\ndef _compute_tfidf_batch(job: _TFIDFComputeJob) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: compute TF-IDF vectors for a batch of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        job: TFIDFComputeJob containing document IDs and required data\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id to (doc_vector, norm) tuple\n    \"\"\"\n    batch_data = {}\n    for doc_id in job.doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = job.doc_term_freqs_dict.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * job.idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        batch_data[doc_id] = (doc_vector, math.sqrt(norm_squared))\n\n    return batch_data\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(query_id: int, query: str, idf_dict: dict, doc_vectors: dict, doc_norms: dict, doc_titles: dict, top_k: int) -> tuple[int, list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: process a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        query_id: ID of the query\n        query: Query string\n        idf_dict: IDF dictionary\n        doc_vectors: Document vectors\n        doc_norms: Document norms\n        doc_titles: Document title mapping\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (query_id, list of SearchResult objects)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf_dict:\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents and compute similarity\n    candidate_scores = {}\n\n    for term in query_vector:\n        query_tfidf = query_vector[term]\n        # Iterate through all documents that have this term\n        for doc_id, doc_vec in doc_vectors.items():\n            if term in doc_vec:\n                if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                    candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n                candidate_scores[doc_id] += query_tfidf * doc_vec[term]\n\n    # Normalize by norms and get top-k\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id, dot_product in candidate_scores.items():\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n        if doc_norm > 0:\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n            doc_id=doc_id,\n            score=score,\n            title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        ))\n\n    return query_id, results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes the CPU-bound operations:\n    1. Document tokenization (embarrassingly parallel)\n    2. TF-IDF vector computation (embarrassingly parallel)\n\n    Global steps (document frequencies, IDF) are computed sequentially\n    on the main process.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of chunks for worker pool (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 1: Parallel tokenization with optimized chunking\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Use dynamic chunking to balance overhead vs. granularity\n    # For large datasets, use larger chunks; for small, use smaller\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n    optimal_chunk_size = max(50, min(chunk_size, num_docs // (num_workers * 2)))\n\n    tokenization_jobs = [\n        _TokenizationJob(doc_id=doc.doc_id, text=doc.title + \" \" + doc.content)\n        for doc in documents\n    ]\n\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n\n    # Use submit + as_completed for better load distribution\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        # Submit all jobs at once to reduce latency\n        futures = {\n            executor.submit(_tokenize_document, job): job.doc_id\n            for job in tokenization_jobs\n        }\n\n        # Process results as they complete to start secondary work early\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            result = future.result()\n            doc_term_freqs[result.doc_id] = result.term_freqs\n            doc_terms[result.doc_id] = result.terms_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(result.terms_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 2: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - small overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 4: Build document vectors and compute norms (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 5: Build inverted index (sequential - acceptable overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform batch similarity search in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes query processing across multiple workers.\n    Each worker independently computes similarity scores for its assigned queries.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Prepare document titles mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Pre-compute query vectors once (shared across all threads)\n    # This amortizes tokenization cost and improves cache locality\n    query_vectors = []\n    query_norms = []\n\n    for query in queries:\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            query_vectors.append({})\n            query_norms.append(0.0)\n            continue\n\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        query_vectors.append(query_vector)\n        query_norms.append(math.sqrt(query_norm_squared))\n\n    results_by_query = [None] * len(queries)\n\n    # Parallelize similarity computation with pre-computed query vectors\n    if len(queries) > 10:\n        # For larger batches, use threading for similarity computation\n        def compute_similarity(query_id):\n            query_vector = query_vectors[query_id]\n            query_norm = query_norms[query_id]\n\n            if not query_vector or query_norm == 0:\n                return query_id, []\n\n            # Find candidates and compute similarity\n            candidate_scores = {}\n            for term, query_tfidf in query_vector.items():\n                for doc_id, doc_vec in index.doc_vectors.items():\n                    if term in doc_vec:\n                        if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                            candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n                        candidate_scores[doc_id] += query_tfidf * doc_vec[term]\n\n            # Normalize and get top-k\n            scores = []\n            for doc_id, dot_product in candidate_scores.items():\n                doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n                if doc_norm > 0:\n                    similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n                    scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n            top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n            results = []\n            for doc_id, score in top_results:\n                results.append(SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n                ))\n\n            return query_id, results\n\n        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            futures = {executor.submit(compute_similarity, qid): qid for qid in range(len(queries))}\n\n            for future in as_completed(futures):\n                query_id, results = future.result()\n                results_by_query[query_id] = results\n    else:\n        # For small batches, sequential is faster\n        for query_id in range(len(queries)):\n            query_vector = query_vectors[query_id]\n            query_norm = query_norms[query_id]\n\n            if not query_vector or query_norm == 0:\n                results_by_query[query_id] = []\n                continue\n\n            candidate_scores = {}\n            for term, query_tfidf in query_vector.items():\n                for doc_id, doc_vec in index.doc_vectors.items():\n                    if term in doc_vec:\n                        if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                            candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n                        candidate_scores[doc_id] += query_tfidf * doc_vec[term]\n\n            scores = []\n            for doc_id, dot_product in candidate_scores.items():\n                doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n                if doc_norm > 0:\n                    similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n                    scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n            top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n            results = []\n            for doc_id, score in top_results:\n                results.append(SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n                ))\n\n            results_by_query[query_id] = results\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_by_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Utility Functions for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef verify_index_equality(index1: TFIDFIndex, index2: TFIDFIndex, tolerance: float = 1e-6) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify that two indices are equivalent (for correctness testing).\n\n    Args:\n        index1: First index\n        index2: Second index\n        tolerance: Floating point tolerance for score comparison\n\n    Returns:\n        True if indices are equivalent\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check basic properties\n    if index1.num_documents != index2.num_documents:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.vocabulary != index2.vocabulary:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.document_frequencies != index2.document_frequencies:\n        return False\n\n    # Check IDF scores\n    for term in index1.idf:\n        if term not in index2.idf:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.idf[term] - index2.idf[term]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    # Check document vectors\n _search_single_query(query_id: int, query: str, idf_dict: dict, doc_vectors: dict, doc_norms: dict, doc_titles: dict, top_k: int) -> tuple[int, list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function: process a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        query_id: ID of the query\n        query: Query string\n        idf_dict: IDF dictionary\n        doc_vectors: Document vectors\n        doc_norms: Document norms\n        doc_titles: Document title mapping\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (query_id, list of SearchResult objects)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf_dict:\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return query_id, []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find term\n        for doc_id, doc_vec in doc_vectors.items():\n            if term in doc_vec:\n                if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                    candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n        if doc_norm > 0:\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(\n ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes the CPU-bound operations:\n    1. Document tokenization (embarrassingly parallel)\n    2. TF-IDF vector computation (embarrassingly parallel)\n\n    Global steps (document frequencies, IDF) are computed sequentially\n    on the main process.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Size of chunks for worker pool (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 1: Parallel tokenization with efficient chunking\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Use larger default chunks to minimize IPC overhead\n    num_docs = len(documents)\n    effective_chunk_size = max(100, chunk_size)\n\n    tokenization_jobs = [\n        _TokenizationJob(doc_id=doc.doc_id, text=doc.title + \" \" + doc.content)\n        for doc in documents\n    ]\n\n    doc_term_freqs:      tokenization_results = executor.map(_tokenize_document, tokenization_jobs, chunksize=effective_chunk_size)\n\n        for result in tokenization_results:\n            doc_term_freqs[result.doc_id] = result.term_freqs\n            doc_terms[result.doc_id] = result.terms_set\n            index.vocabulary.update(result.terms_set)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 2: Calculate document frequencies (sequential - small overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 4: Build document vectors and compute norms (sequential - fast)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # STEP 5: Build inverted index (sequential - acceptable overhead)\n    # ========================================================================\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform batch similarity search in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes query processing across multiple workers.\n    Each worker independently computes similarity scores for its assigned queries.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        query_vectors.append(query_vector)\n        query_norms.append(math.sqrt(query_norm_squared))\n\n    results_by_query = [None] * len(queries)\n\n    # Parallelize similarity computation with pre-computed query vectors\n    if len(queries) > 10:\n        # For larger batches, use threading for similarity computation\n        def compute_similarity(query_id):\n            query_vector = query_vectors[query_id]\n            query_norm = query_norms[query_id]\n\n            if not query_vector or query_norm == 0:\n                return query_id, []\n\n            # Find candidates and compute similarity\n            candidate_scores = {}\n            for term, query_tfidf in query_vector.items():\n                for doc_id, doc_vec in index.doc_vectors.items():\n                    if term in doc_vec:\n                        if doc_id not in candidate_scores:\n                            candidate_scores[doc_id] = 0.0\n                        candidate_scores[doc_id] += query_tfidf * doc_vec[term]\n\n            # Normalize and get top-k\n            scores = []\n            for doc_id, dot_product in candidate_scores.items():\n                doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n                if doc_norm > 0:\n x[1])\n\n            results = []\n            for doc_id, score in top_results:\n                results.append(SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n                ))\n\n            return query_id, results\n\n        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            futures = {executor.submit(compute_similarity, qid): qid for qid in range(len(queries))}\n\n            for future in as_completed(futures):\n                query_id, results = future.result()\n                results_by_query[query_id] = results\n    else:\n        # For small batches, sequential is faster\n        for query_id in range(len(queries)):\n            query_vector = query_vectors[query_id]\n            query_norm = query_norms[query_id]\n\n            if not query_vector or query_norm == 0:\n                results_by_query[query_id] = []\n query_tfidf * doc_vec[term]\n\n            scores = []\n            for doc_id, dot_product in candidate_scores.items():\n                doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n                if score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n                ))\n\n            results_by_query[query_id] = results\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - ============================================================================\n\n\ndef verify_index_equality(index1: TFIDFIndex, index2: TFIDFIndex, tolerance: float = 1e-6) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify that two indices are equivalent (for correctness testing).\n\n    Args:\n        index1: First index\n        index2: Second index\n        tolerance: Floating point tolerance for score comparison\n\n    Returns:\n        True if indices are equivalent\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check basic properties\n    if index1.num_documents != index2.num_documents:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.vocabulary != index2.vocabulary:\n        return False\n\n    if index1.document_frequencies != index2.document_frequencies:\n        return False\n\n    # Check IDF scores\n    for term in index1.idf:\n        if term not in index2.idf:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.idf[term] - index2.idf[term]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    # Check document vectors\n    if set(index1.doc_vectors.keys()) != set(index2.doc_vectors.keys()):\n        return False\n\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_vectors:\n        vec1 = index1.doc_vectors[doc_id]\n        vec2 = index2.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n\n        if set(vec1.keys()) != set(vec2.keys()):\n            return False\n\n        for term in vec1:\n            if abs(vec1[term] - vec2.get(term, 0)) > tolerance:\n                return False\n\n    # Check document norms\n    for doc_id in index1.doc_norms:\n        if doc_id not in index2.doc_norms:\n            return False\n        if abs(index1.doc_norms[doc_id] - index2.doc_norms[doc_id]) > tolerance:\n            return False\n\n    return True\n\n\n#
